# Audit — 2026-09-22 (open defects)

Independent audit of the tree as left by the packet workflow (tag
`archive/pre-audit-2026-09-22`, commit `0adc7aa`). Four area audits read the
code and ran the real bundles: backend over HTTP/WS, the editor in headless
Chrome, a scripted Beacon Reach playthrough on real Rapier, a standalone
export, and the MCP server over stdio.

## Summary

The foundations are real: the command pipeline (revision conflicts,
idempotent retries, atomic writes, undo as new transactions), module
boundaries, Rapier physics, keyboard/gamepad input, the fixed-step runtime,
the export bundle's independence from editor/server code, and a real MCP
server. But the product was never exercised as a user would use it. Every
milestone gate was a self-review; all browser, audio, and input checks were
left "UNVERIFIED"; no test touched the React app, viewport, gizmo, or
browser session client. At the audit the editor could not be used for
editing, and M2–M4 features were reachable only through test harnesses.

Build: green. Tests: 2252/2255 (the 3 failures were a brittle build-script
fixture test, now retired).

## Defects

Severity: C critical, H high, M medium, L low. Status: `open`, `fixed <commit>`
(the commit that fixed it), `superseded` (the subject is gone, or `→ Dn`: a
duplicate or the row that carries it on), `won't fix` (with the reason), or
`watch` (seen once, cause unknown, kept to catch a recurrence). A new row starts
`open` with the next free number; whoever fixes it sets the status. The counts are in `docs/STATUS.md`.

This file holds the open and watch rows. Closed rows (fixed, superseded, won't fix) are in
`docs/audit-closed.md`, with the D-numbers they always had; a row closed here moves there at the next tidy
(at the latest when its phase ends). The last D-number in use may be in either file.

Reserved: D430–D499 for branch phase-45
Reserved: D500–D599 for branch phase-45 (added on the branch while main is frozen; main skips it at the merge)
Reserved: D600–D699 for branch phase-45 (added on the branch while main is frozen; main skips it at the merge)
Reserved: D700–D799 for branch phase-45 (added on the branch while main is frozen; main skips it at the merge)
Reserved: D800–D899 for branch phase-45 (added on the branch while main is frozen; main skips it at the merge)

At 0.667 (1280×720 → 1920×1080) every class holds the cap and TAAU is cheaper than SMAA + FSR 1 (village 5.49 vs 5.86, interior 8.87 vs 9.03, landscape 11.34 vs 12.06). The landscape's resolve (1.19–1.37 ms) misses the ≤ 1 ms target. Performance phase: decimate far meshes, cull far draws, mesh cheaper 46d.13c-1 (Play, medium, 8 km world): the bricks' drawn set did not change during the 2.4 km flight (no far group swapped in within 40 s while the near field streamed); the mesh fallback's flight ran the editor + Play browser out of its 6 GB scope (OOM-killed, both renderers; frame heap 1.83 GB before it) 46d.13c-3: the bricks (the default) follow the flight: far jobs are the pool's background jobs with ≥ 1/4 of the workers, nodes made where the error is largest; Play medium WebGPU flight swaps 0 → 255 (3dff0720) → 543 groups (7a27b8a9), 1–2 missed. The mesh fallback's cost and its flight OOM carried to D581 (46d.14) | `packages/voxel/src/far-mesh.ts`, `packages/three-adapter/src/voxel-far-mesh.ts` |
